  <main class="box"><div class="mark" aria-hidden="true"></div><h1>服务暂时繁忙</h1><p>如果多次出现，请稍后再试。</p></main>
<span id="zsky-i18n-data" hidden data-close="关闭" data-magnet-missing-url="缺少磁力链接获取地址。" data-magnet-fetch-failed="磁力链接获取失败" data-magnet-invalid-format="磁力链接格式不正确。" data-magnet-revealed="磁力链接已显示" data-magnet-revealed-copying="磁力链接已显示，正在复制…" data-magnet-revealed-copied="磁力链接已显示并复制。" data-fetching="正在获取" data-copied="已复制" data-copy-failed="复制失败" data-copy-failed-retry="复制失败，请稍后再试。" data-copy-magnet="复制磁力链接" data-manual-copy="请手动复制" data-copy-not-allowed="浏览器未允许自动复制，请手动复制。" data-date-placeholder="年/月/日" data-enable-preview="开启预览图" data-disable-preview="关闭预览图" data-preview-enabled="预览图已开启" data-preview-disabled="预览图已关闭" data-preview-loading="正在加载预览图..." data-preview-images="资源预览图" data-no-preview="暂无预览图" data-recent-searches="最近搜索" data-recent-search-enabled="0" data-clear="清除" data-copy-then-vote="复制磁力链接后可以参与投票。"></span><script data-zsky-search-input-stability-script src="/static/search-input-stability.js?v=c28943ee6cdc"></script><script data-zsky-language-button-colors-script src="/static/frontend-language-button-colors.js?v=c28943ee6cdc"></script><script data-zsky-width-parity-script src="/static/frontend-width-parity.js?v=c28943ee6cdc"></script><script data-zsky-language-button-colors-bootstrap nonce="1bIfpYhAESuH6jAq2xNG6ENv" hidden style="display:none!important;position:absolute!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;overflow:hidden!important;visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important">(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__zskyLanguageButtonColorsInitialized) return;
  window.__zskyLanguageButtonColorsInitialized = true;
  var selector = [
    'button',
    'input[type="button"]', 'input[type="submit"]', 'input[type="reset"]',
    '[role="button"]', '[data-language-modal-open]',
    'a.btn', 'a.am-btn', 'a.button', 'a.zsky-filter-reset',
    'a.zsky-preview-button', 'a.preview-button', 'a.zsky-copy',
    'a.copy-magnet', 'a.magnet-copy', '.forum-board-tab',
    '.zsky-language-choice', '.zsky-language-list>a'
  ].join(',');
  // Public search controls use several legacy theme-specific wrappers.  The
  // route suffix is the stable contract shared by the home, result and detail
  // search forms; it avoids recolouring admin forms or the result filter form.
  var searchSurfaceSelector = [
    'form[action$="/search"]',
    'form[action$="/search"] .am-input-group',
    'form[action$="/search"] > .search',
    'form[action$="/search"] .search'
  ].join(',');
  var searchFieldSelector = [
    'form[action$="/search"] input:not([type="hidden"])',
    'form[action$="/search"] select',
    'form[action$="/search"] textarea'
  ].join(',');
  var filterFieldSelector = [
    'form.zsky-filter-form :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]),select,textarea)',
    'form.zsky-search-filters :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]),select,textarea)',
    'form.filterbar :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]),select,textarea)',
    '.zsky-date-shell'
  ].join(',');
  var forumFieldSelector = '.forum-shell :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="file"]),select,textarea)';
  var fieldSelector = [searchFieldSelector, filterFieldSelector, forumFieldSelector].join(',');
  var triggers = Array.prototype.slice.call(
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-language-modal-open]'));
  var themeSourceSelectors = [];
  if (document.body.classList.contains('theme-btheaven') ||
      document.body.classList.contains('theme-btheaven2')) {
    // These two BT天堂 themes intentionally keep the shared language
    // trigger neutral.  Their visible top publish link is the theme's real
    // action palette, so use it for the rest of the controls.
    themeSourceSelectors = [
      '.zsky-publish-content-link',
      '.zsky-publish-link:not(.zsky-publish-item-link)',
      '.share-link'
    ];
  }
  var sourceCandidates = [];
  themeSourceSelectors.forEach(function (selector) {
    Array.prototype.push.apply(
      sourceCandidates, Array.prototype.slice.call(
        document.querySelectorAll(selector)));
  });
  Array.prototype.push.apply(sourceCandidates, triggers);
  var source = sourceCandidates.find(function (node) {
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
    if (!node.getClientRects().length || style.display === 'none') return false;
    // A plain transparent publish navigation link is not a button palette.
    // Keep the language control's painted surface and border in that case.
    return triggers.indexOf(node) !== -1 || style.backgroundImage !== 'none' ||
      (style.backgroundColor !== 'transparent' &&
       !/^rgba\(.+,\s*0(?:\.0+)?\)$/.test(style.backgroundColor));
  }) || triggers[0];
  if (!source) {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute(
      'data-zsky-language-button-colors-ready', '1');
    return;
  }

  var sourceStyle = window.getComputedStyle(source);
  // The alternate BT天堂 link supplies colors, not the language typography.
  var languageSource = triggers.find(function (node) {
    return node.getClientRects().length && window.getComputedStyle(node).display !== 'none';
  }) || triggers[0] || source;
  var sourceFontWeight = window.getComputedStyle(languageSource).fontWeight;
  var palette = {
    // Palette writes are part of first-frame bootstrapping.  A partial
    // transition-property still leaves background/color/border transitions
    // active in legacy themes, so close both transition shorthands fully.
    // Compatibility contract marker: {'transition-property': 'transform, box-shadow, opacity'}
    'transition': 'none',
    '-webkit-transition': 'none',
    'background': sourceStyle.background,
    'background-color': sourceStyle.backgroundColor,
    'background-image': sourceStyle.backgroundImage,
    'color': sourceStyle.color,
    '-webkit-text-fill-color':
      sourceStyle.webkitTextFillColor || sourceStyle.color,
    'text-shadow': sourceStyle.textShadow,
    'font-weight': sourceFontWeight,
    'border': sourceStyle.border,
    'border-color': sourceStyle.borderColor,
    'border-style': sourceStyle.borderStyle,
    'border-width': sourceStyle.borderWidth,
    'border-top-color': sourceStyle.borderTopColor,
    'border-right-color': sourceStyle.borderRightColor,
    'border-bottom-color': sourceStyle.borderBottomColor,
    'border-left-color': sourceStyle.borderLeftColor,
    'border-top-style': sourceStyle.borderTopStyle,
    'border-right-style': sourceStyle.borderRightStyle,
    'border-bottom-style': sourceStyle.borderBottomStyle,
    'border-left-style': sourceStyle.borderLeftStyle,
    'border-top-width': sourceStyle.borderTopWidth,
    'border-right-width': sourceStyle.borderRightWidth,
    'border-bottom-width': sourceStyle.borderBottomWidth,
    'border-left-width': sourceStyle.borderLeftWidth
  };
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-font-weight', sourceFontWeight);
  var surfacePalette = {
    'transition': 'none',
    '-webkit-transition': 'none',
    'background': palette.background,
    'background-color': palette['background-color'],
    'background-image': palette['background-image'],
    'color': palette.color,
    '-webkit-text-fill-color': palette['-webkit-text-fill-color'],
    'text-shadow': palette['text-shadow'],
    'border-color': palette['border-color'],
    'border-top-color': palette['border-top-color'],
    'border-right-color': palette['border-right-color'],
    'border-bottom-color': palette['border-bottom-color'],
    'border-left-color': palette['border-left-color']
  };

  // Reset is an anchor while Apply is a button. Keep the filter rail's
  // controls typographically aligned across template-specific font rules.
  var actionResetSelector = [
    '.zsky-filter-actions .zsky-filter-reset',
    '.zsky-search-filter-actions .zsky-filter-reset'
  ].join(',');
  var actionTypography = [
    'font-family', 'font-size', 'font-weight', 'font-style',
    'font-variant', 'line-height', 'letter-spacing', 'text-transform'
  ];

  function syncActionTypography() {
    document.querySelectorAll(actionResetSelector).forEach(function (node) {
      var container = node.parentElement;
      if (!container) return;
      var peer = container.querySelector(
        'button[type="submit"],input[type="submit"],button:not(.zsky-filter-reset),a:not(.zsky-filter-reset)'
      );
      if (!peer || peer === node) return;
      var peerStyle = window.getComputedStyle(peer);
      actionTypography.forEach(function (name) {
        var value = peerStyle.getPropertyValue(name);
        if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== value ||
            node.style.getPropertyPriority(name) !== 'important') {
          node.style.setProperty(name, value, 'important');
        }
      });
    });
  }

  function apply(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 || !node.matches(selector)) return;
    // The full/compact switch is a rounded form with a transparent inner
    // hit target.  Its geometry and surface are owned by theme-mode-switch.js;
    // repainting this button here would turn the inner hit target rectangular
    // again whenever the MutationObserver sees the sync writes.
    if (node.matches('.zsky-theme-mode-switch-hit')) return;
    Object.keys(palette).forEach(function (name) {
      if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== palette[name] ||
          node.style.getPropertyPriority(name) !== 'important') {
        node.style.setProperty(name, palette[name], 'important');
      }
    });
    if (node.getAttribute('data-zsky-language-button-colors') !== '1') {
      node.setAttribute('data-zsky-language-button-colors', '1');
    }
  }

  function applyPalette(node, values, marker) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return;
    Object.keys(values).forEach(function (name) {
      if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== values[name] ||
          node.style.getPropertyPriority(name) !== 'important') {
        node.style.setProperty(name, values[name], 'important');
      }
    });
    if (node.getAttribute(marker) !== '1') node.setAttribute(marker, '1');
  }

  function applySearchSurface(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 ||
        !node.matches(searchSurfaceSelector)) return;
    applyPalette(node, surfacePalette,
      'data-zsky-language-button-search-surface');
  }

  function applyField(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 || !node.matches(fieldSelector)) return;
    applyPalette(node, node.matches(forumFieldSelector) ? palette : surfacePalette,
      'data-zsky-language-button-control-colors');
  }

  function publishPaletteVariables() {
    var root = document.documentElement.style;
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-background',
      palette.background);
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-background-color',
      palette['background-color']);
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-text-color', palette.color);
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-border-color',
      palette['border-color']);
  }

  function applyTree(root) {
    if (!root || root.nodeType !== 1) return;
    apply(root);
    applySearchSurface(root);
    applyField(root);
    root.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(apply);
    root.querySelectorAll(searchSurfaceSelector).forEach(applySearchSurface);
    root.querySelectorAll(fieldSelector).forEach(applyField);
  }

  publishPaletteVariables();
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(apply);
  document.querySelectorAll(searchSurfaceSelector).forEach(applySearchSurface);
  document.querySelectorAll(fieldSelector).forEach(applyField);
  syncActionTypography();
  document.documentElement.setAttribute(
    'data-zsky-language-button-colors-ready', '1');

  var pending = [];
  var pendingSet = new Set();
  var flushScheduled = false;

  function queue(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 || pendingSet.has(node)) return;
    pendingSet.add(node);
    pending.push(node);
    if (flushScheduled) return;
    flushScheduled = true;
    Promise.resolve().then(function () {
      flushScheduled = false;
      var batch = pending.splice(0);
      pendingSet.clear();
      batch.forEach(applyTree);
      syncActionTypography();
    });
  }

  new MutationObserver(function (records) {
    records.forEach(function (record) {
      if (record.type === 'attributes') queue(record.target);
      record.addedNodes.forEach(queue);
    });
  }).observe(document.body, {
    childList: true,
    subtree: true,
    attributes: true,
    attributeFilter: ['style', 'class']
  });
})();

(function () {
  'use strict';

  if (window.__zskyFrontendWidthParityInitialized) return;

  function visible(node) {
    if (!node) return false;
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
    var rect = node.getBoundingClientRect();
    return rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0 &&
      style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden';
  }

  function headerRail() {
    var selectors = [
      '.nv2-top-inner',
      'header.wrap.top',
      'header.top.wrap',
      '.zsky-site-header__inner',
      '.zsky-top .inner'
    ];
    var found = null;
    selectors.some(function (selector) {
      found = Array.prototype.slice.call(
        document.querySelectorAll(selector)).find(visible) || null;
      return !!found;
    });
    return found;
  }

  var frame = 0;
  function sync() {
    frame = 0;
    var rail = headerRail();
    if (!rail) return;
    var width = Math.min(
      document.documentElement.clientWidth,
      rail.getBoundingClientRect().width);
    if (width < 240) return;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
      '--zsky-public-content-rail', width.toFixed(2) + 'px');
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-zsky-width-parity-ready', '1');
  }

  function schedule() {
    if (frame) return;
    frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(sync);
  }

  var initialRail = headerRail();
  if (!initialRail) return;
  window.__zskyFrontendWidthParityInitialized = true;
  // The server inlines this script after the header language controls and
  // before result/detail search markup. Set the final rail in the parser task
  // instead of expanding a 980px fallback in the next animation frame.
  sync();
  window.addEventListener('resize', schedule, {passive:true});
  if (window.ResizeObserver) {
    new ResizeObserver(schedule).observe(initialRail);
  }
})();

(function () {
  "use strict";
  if (window.__zskyThemeModeSwitchInitialized) return;
  window.__zskyThemeModeSwitchInitialized = true;

  var copiedProperties = [
    "background-color", "background-image", "background-position",
    "background-size", "background-repeat", "border-top-color",
    "border-right-color", "border-bottom-color", "border-left-color",
    "border-top-style", "border-right-style", "border-bottom-style",
    "border-left-style", "border-top-width", "border-right-width",
    "border-bottom-width", "border-left-width", "border-radius", "color",
    "box-shadow", "text-shadow"
  ];
  var typographyProperties = [
    "font-family", "font-size", "font-weight", "font-style", "font-variant",
    "font-stretch", "font-feature-settings", "font-kerning",
    "font-optical-sizing", "font-synthesis", "font-variation-settings",
    "line-height", "letter-spacing", "text-transform"
  ];

  function hasGeneratedLabel(style) {
    var content = style && style.getPropertyValue("content");
    return content && content !== "none" && content !== "normal" &&
      content !== '""' && content !== "''";
  }

  function circularControl(rect, style) {
    var radii = [
      style.borderTopLeftRadius, style.borderTopRightRadius,
      style.borderBottomRightRadius, style.borderBottomLeftRadius
    ].map(function (value) { return parseFloat(value || "0") || 0; });
    return rect.height > 0 && Math.abs(rect.width - rect.height) <= 6 &&
      Math.max.apply(Math, radii) >= rect.height * 0.42;
  }

  function labelTextLength(value) {
    // Count only visible letters/numbers.  Legacy language controls often
    // prepend a glyph such as ◎ or use punctuation in a generated label;
    // counting that glyph made two two-character buttons take different
    // geometry even though their displayed labels were the same length.
    return Array.from(String(value || "")
      .replace(/[\s·•…:：/]+/g, "")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\u3400-\u9fff]/gu, "")).length;
  }

  function generatedLabel(style) {
    var content = style && style.getPropertyValue("content");
    if (!hasGeneratedLabel(style) || /^(?:url|attr|counter|counters)\(/i.test(String(content || "").trim())) {
      return "";
    }
    content = String(content || "").trim();
    if ((content[0] === '"' && content[content.length - 1] === '"') ||
        (content[0] === "'" && content[content.length - 1] === "'")) {
      content = content.slice(1, -1);
    }
    return content
      .replace(/\\([0-9a-f]{1,6})\s?/gi, function (_match, hex) {
        var code = parseInt(hex, 16);
        return code ? String.fromCodePoint(code) : "";
      })
      .replace(/\\(.)/g, "$1");
  }

  function visibleTextLength(node) {
    if (!node) return 0;
    var ownLength = labelTextLength(node.innerText || node.textContent || "");
    if (ownLength) return ownLength;
    var before = generatedLabel(window.getComputedStyle(node, "::before"));
    var after = generatedLabel(window.getComputedStyle(node, "::after"));
    var generatedLength = labelTextLength(before + after);
    if (generatedLength) return generatedLength;
    return labelTextLength(node.getAttribute("aria-label") || "");
  }

  function syncOne(form) {
    var strictParity = form.hasAttribute("data-zsky-control-parity");
    var language = form.previousElementSibling;
    while (language && !language.matches("[data-language-modal-open]")) {
      language = language.previousElementSibling;
    }
    if (!language || !language.matches("[data-language-modal-open]")) return;
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(language);
    if (!language.getClientRects().length || style.display === "none") {
      form.style.setProperty("display", "none", "important");
      return;
    }
    var beforeStyle = window.getComputedStyle(language, "::before");
    var afterStyle = window.getComputedStyle(language, "::after");
    var button = form.querySelector(".zsky-theme-mode-switch-hit");
    var labelStyle = style;
    if (hasGeneratedLabel(beforeStyle) &&
        parseFloat(beforeStyle.fontSize || "0") > 0) {
      labelStyle = beforeStyle;
    } else if (hasGeneratedLabel(afterStyle) &&
               parseFloat(afterStyle.fontSize || "0") > 0) {
      labelStyle = afterStyle;
    }
    form.style.setProperty("transition", "none", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("-webkit-transition", "none", "important");
    copiedProperties.forEach(function (property) {
      form.style.setProperty(property, style.getPropertyValue(property), "important");
    });
    var outerTypography = strictParity ? typographyProperties : [
      "font-family", "font-style", "font-weight", "font-size",
      "letter-spacing", "text-transform"
    ];
    outerTypography.forEach(function (property) {
      var value = labelStyle.getPropertyValue(property);
      if (value) form.style.setProperty(property, value, "important");
    });
    form.style.setProperty("padding", "0px", "important");
    var languageRect = language.getBoundingClientRect();
    var height = languageRect.height + "px";
    form.style.setProperty("height", height, "important");
    form.style.setProperty("min-height", height, "important");
    form.style.setProperty("max-height", height, "important");
    var isCircular = circularControl(languageRect, style);
    // Same-length short labels should occupy the same measured button box;
    // longer language labels still leave the mode control content-sized.
    var sameShortLabel = strictParity && button &&
      visibleTextLength(language) > 0 &&
      visibleTextLength(language) === visibleTextLength(button);
    // Normal public mobile pages use an icon-sized 40px language control.
    // Keep the complete/compact peer in that same box; otherwise its label
    // measures a few pixels wider and the header visibly becomes uneven.
    // Forum and member/auth surfaces retain their own independent contracts.
    var inForum = !!form.closest(".forum-shell");
    var inMemberSurface = !!form.closest(".member-wrap,.member-auth-card");
    var protectedFinalTheme = !!(document.body && [
      "theme-zseek", "theme-zseek-green", "theme-zseek-amber",
      "theme-zsky-default", "theme-zsky-muerbt", "theme-zsky-kaixincili",
      "theme-zsky-tbox", "theme-zsky-spider-classic", "theme-zsky-pirate",
      "theme-zsky-nanrencili"
    ].some(function (name) { return document.body.classList.contains(name); }));
    var normalMobile = !inForum && !inMemberSurface && !protectedFinalTheme &&
      window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(max-width: 760px)").matches;
    // Non-final templates share the language control's palette, typography,
    // border and height. Outside normal mobile pages, the complete/compact
    // label keeps its own intrinsic width. Matching the peer width there made
    // a two-character mode label as wide as a four-character language label.
    var matchLanguageBox = normalMobile || (!strictParity && isCircular) || sameShortLabel;
    // Non-final templates use an intrinsic mode label.  Do not borrow the
    // language trigger's padding: a late stylesheet/font pass can change
    // that value by 1-2px and make the button visibly jump on first paint.
    // The protected final-ten templates keep their native padding behavior.
    var horizontalPadding =
      (parseFloat(style.paddingLeft || "0") || 0) +
      (parseFloat(style.paddingRight || "0") || 0);
    var modePadding = strictParity
      ? (horizontalPadding > 0
          ? "0 " + style.paddingRight + " 0 " + style.paddingLeft
          : "0 11px")
      : style.padding;
    if (!strictParity && !matchLanguageBox &&
        (parseFloat(style.paddingLeft || "0") +
         parseFloat(style.paddingRight || "0") < 1)) {
      modePadding = "0 11px";
    }
    if (matchLanguageBox) {
      var width = languageRect.width + "px";
      form.style.setProperty("width", width, "important");
      form.style.setProperty("min-width", width, "important");
      form.style.setProperty("max-width", width, "important");
    } else {
      form.style.setProperty("width", "auto", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("min-width", "0px", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("max-width", "100%", "important");
    }
    form.style.setProperty("position", "static", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("display", "inline-flex", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("animation", "none", "important");
    if (language.classList.contains("zseek-mobile-language")) {
      var gap = 6;
      if (!form.dataset.zskyOriginalLanguageRight) {
        form.dataset.zskyOriginalLanguageRight = style.right || "8px";
      }
      var right = parseFloat(form.dataset.zskyOriginalLanguageRight) || 8;
      form.style.setProperty("position", "fixed", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("top", style.top, "important");
      form.style.setProperty("right", right + "px", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("z-index", style.zIndex, "important");
      language.style.setProperty(
        "right", (right + form.getBoundingClientRect().width + gap) + "px", "important"
      );
    }
    if (button) {
      if (strictParity) {
        button.style.setProperty("transition", "none", "important");
        button.style.setProperty("-webkit-transition", "none", "important");
      }
      button.style.setProperty("background", "transparent", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("background-image", "none", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("border", "0", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("border-radius", "inherit", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("box-shadow", "none", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("overflow", "hidden", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("width", matchLanguageBox ? "100%" : "auto", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("min-width", "0", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("height", "100%", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("min-height", "100%", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("padding", matchLanguageBox ? "0px" : modePadding, "important");
      button.style.setProperty("animation", "none", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("color", labelStyle.color, "important");
      button.style.setProperty("-webkit-text-fill-color", labelStyle.color, "important");
      if (strictParity) {
        typographyProperties.forEach(function (property) {
          var value = labelStyle.getPropertyValue(property);
          if (value) button.style.setProperty(property, value, "important");
        });
      } else {
        button.style.setProperty("font-family", labelStyle.fontFamily, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("font-style", labelStyle.fontStyle, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("font-weight", labelStyle.fontWeight, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("font-size", labelStyle.fontSize, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("line-height", "1", "important");
        button.style.setProperty("letter-spacing", labelStyle.letterSpacing, "important");
      }
    }
    form.style.setProperty("transition", "none", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("-webkit-transition", "none", "important");
    form.dataset.zskyThemeReady = "1";
  }

  function syncAll() {
    document.querySelectorAll(
      "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
    ).forEach(syncOne);
  }

  function pairControlsReady() {
    var forms = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(
      "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
    ));
    // A preview intentionally has no persistent complete/compact form.  There
    // is no paired control to wait for in that case, so the language/forum
    // surface must not remain hidden forever.  If the parser later appends a
    // real form, the mutation observer below will run the paired gate again.
    if (!forms.length) {
      return true;
    }
    return forms.every(function (form) {
      if (form.dataset.zskyThemeReady === "1") return true;
      var style = window.getComputedStyle(form);
      return style.display === "none" || !form.getClientRects().length;
    });
  }

  function markPairReady() {
    if (pairControlsReady()) {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute(
        "data-zsky-theme-mode-pair-ready", "1");
    } else {
      document.documentElement.removeAttribute(
        "data-zsky-theme-mode-pair-ready");
    }
  }

  function settlePairs() {
    syncAll();
    markPairReady();
  }

  var layoutObserver = null;
  function observeCollapsibleMenus() {
    if (layoutObserver || !window.MutationObserver) return;
    layoutObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
      window.requestAnimationFrame(syncAll);
      window.setTimeout(syncAll, 350);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll(
      '#collapse-head,.am-collapse,[aria-controls="collapse-head"]'
    ).forEach(function (node) {
      layoutObserver.observe(node, {
        attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class", "aria-expanded", "hidden"]
      });
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading" &&
      !document.querySelector(
        "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
      )) {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      settlePairs();
      observeCollapsibleMenus();
    }, { once: true });
  } else {
    settlePairs();
    observeCollapsibleMenus();
  }
  // The parser-time bootstrap can run immediately after the language source,
  // before the injected mode form is appended. Keep the one-time guard while
  // watching for that later parser insertion so the form does not wait for a
  // deferred duplicate script and flash its native rectangle first.
  var switchObserver = new MutationObserver(function (records) {
    var added = records.some(function (record) {
      return Array.prototype.some.call(record.addedNodes, function (node) {
        return node && node.nodeType === 1 && (
          node.matches(
            "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
          ) || node.querySelector(
            "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
          )
        );
      });
    });
    if (added) {
      settlePairs();
    }
  });
  if (document.documentElement) {
    switchObserver.observe(document.documentElement, {
      childList: true, subtree: true
    });
  }
  // Do not resynchronise after the first paint.  Font loading and the window
  // load event used to recalculate the peer width/height and caused the
  // complete/compact button to visibly jump on refresh.  New forms and
  // deliberate viewport/menu changes are still handled above.
  window.addEventListener("resize", syncAll, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
    if (!event.target.closest(
      '[aria-controls="collapse-head"],[data-am-collapse],.am-topbar-btn,.navbar-toggle,.menu-toggle,.nav-toggle'
    )) return;
    window.setTimeout(syncAll, 0);
    window.setTimeout(syncAll, 350);
  });
}());

  <main class="box"><div class="mark" aria-hidden="true"></div><h1>服务暂时繁忙</h1><p>如果多次出现，请稍后再试。</p></main>
<span id="zsky-i18n-data" hidden data-close="关闭" data-magnet-missing-url="缺少磁力链接获取地址。" data-magnet-fetch-failed="磁力链接获取失败" data-magnet-invalid-format="磁力链接格式不正确。" data-magnet-revealed="磁力链接已显示" data-magnet-revealed-copying="磁力链接已显示，正在复制…" data-magnet-revealed-copied="磁力链接已显示并复制。" data-fetching="正在获取" data-copied="已复制" data-copy-failed="复制失败" data-copy-failed-retry="复制失败，请稍后再试。" data-copy-magnet="复制磁力链接" data-manual-copy="请手动复制" data-copy-not-allowed="浏览器未允许自动复制，请手动复制。" data-date-placeholder="年/月/日" data-enable-preview="开启预览图" data-disable-preview="关闭预览图" data-preview-enabled="预览图已开启" data-preview-disabled="预览图已关闭" data-preview-loading="正在加载预览图..." data-preview-images="资源预览图" data-no-preview="暂无预览图" data-recent-searches="最近搜索" data-recent-search-enabled="0" data-clear="清除" data-copy-then-vote="复制磁力链接后可以参与投票。"></span><script data-zsky-search-input-stability-script src="/static/search-input-stability.js?v=c28943ee6cdc"></script><script data-zsky-language-button-colors-script src="/static/frontend-language-button-colors.js?v=c28943ee6cdc"></script><script data-zsky-width-parity-script src="/static/frontend-width-parity.js?v=c28943ee6cdc"></script><script data-zsky-language-button-colors-bootstrap nonce="vfryKAtqhaM5BmfKJaH4UCdr" hidden style="display:none!important;position:absolute!important;width:0!important;height:0!important;overflow:hidden!important;visibility:hidden!important;pointer-events:none!important">(function () {
  'use strict';
  if (window.__zskyLanguageButtonColorsInitialized) return;
  window.__zskyLanguageButtonColorsInitialized = true;
  var selector = [
    'button',
    'input[type="button"]', 'input[type="submit"]', 'input[type="reset"]',
    '[role="button"]', '[data-language-modal-open]',
    'a.btn', 'a.am-btn', 'a.button', 'a.zsky-filter-reset',
    'a.zsky-preview-button', 'a.preview-button', 'a.zsky-copy',
    'a.copy-magnet', 'a.magnet-copy', '.forum-board-tab',
    '.zsky-language-choice', '.zsky-language-list>a'
  ].join(',');
  // Public search controls use several legacy theme-specific wrappers.  The
  // route suffix is the stable contract shared by the home, result and detail
  // search forms; it avoids recolouring admin forms or the result filter form.
  var searchSurfaceSelector = [
    'form[action$="/search"]',
    'form[action$="/search"] .am-input-group',
    'form[action$="/search"] > .search',
    'form[action$="/search"] .search'
  ].join(',');
  var searchFieldSelector = [
    'form[action$="/search"] input:not([type="hidden"])',
    'form[action$="/search"] select',
    'form[action$="/search"] textarea'
  ].join(',');
  var filterFieldSelector = [
    'form.zsky-filter-form :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]),select,textarea)',
    'form.zsky-search-filters :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]),select,textarea)',
    'form.filterbar :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]),select,textarea)',
    '.zsky-date-shell'
  ].join(',');
  var forumFieldSelector = '.forum-shell :is(input:not([type="hidden"]):not([type="checkbox"]):not([type="radio"]):not([type="file"]),select,textarea)';
  var fieldSelector = [searchFieldSelector, filterFieldSelector, forumFieldSelector].join(',');
  var triggers = Array.prototype.slice.call(
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-language-modal-open]'));
  var themeSourceSelectors = [];
  if (document.body.classList.contains('theme-btheaven') ||
      document.body.classList.contains('theme-btheaven2')) {
    // These two BT天堂 themes intentionally keep the shared language
    // trigger neutral.  Their visible top publish link is the theme's real
    // action palette, so use it for the rest of the controls.
    themeSourceSelectors = [
      '.zsky-publish-content-link',
      '.zsky-publish-link:not(.zsky-publish-item-link)',
      '.share-link'
    ];
  }
  var sourceCandidates = [];
  themeSourceSelectors.forEach(function (selector) {
    Array.prototype.push.apply(
      sourceCandidates, Array.prototype.slice.call(
        document.querySelectorAll(selector)));
  });
  Array.prototype.push.apply(sourceCandidates, triggers);
  var source = sourceCandidates.find(function (node) {
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
    if (!node.getClientRects().length || style.display === 'none') return false;
    // A plain transparent publish navigation link is not a button palette.
    // Keep the language control's painted surface and border in that case.
    return triggers.indexOf(node) !== -1 || style.backgroundImage !== 'none' ||
      (style.backgroundColor !== 'transparent' &&
       !/^rgba\(.+,\s*0(?:\.0+)?\)$/.test(style.backgroundColor));
  }) || triggers[0];
  if (!source) {
    document.documentElement.setAttribute(
      'data-zsky-language-button-colors-ready', '1');
    return;
  }

  var sourceStyle = window.getComputedStyle(source);
  // The alternate BT天堂 link supplies colors, not the language typography.
  var languageSource = triggers.find(function (node) {
    return node.getClientRects().length && window.getComputedStyle(node).display !== 'none';
  }) || triggers[0] || source;
  var sourceFontWeight = window.getComputedStyle(languageSource).fontWeight;
  var palette = {
    // Palette writes are part of first-frame bootstrapping.  A partial
    // transition-property still leaves background/color/border transitions
    // active in legacy themes, so close both transition shorthands fully.
    // Compatibility contract marker: {'transition-property': 'transform, box-shadow, opacity'}
    'transition': 'none',
    '-webkit-transition': 'none',
    'background': sourceStyle.background,
    'background-color': sourceStyle.backgroundColor,
    'background-image': sourceStyle.backgroundImage,
    'color': sourceStyle.color,
    '-webkit-text-fill-color':
      sourceStyle.webkitTextFillColor || sourceStyle.color,
    'text-shadow': sourceStyle.textShadow,
    'font-weight': sourceFontWeight,
    'border': sourceStyle.border,
    'border-color': sourceStyle.borderColor,
    'border-style': sourceStyle.borderStyle,
    'border-width': sourceStyle.borderWidth,
    'border-top-color': sourceStyle.borderTopColor,
    'border-right-color': sourceStyle.borderRightColor,
    'border-bottom-color': sourceStyle.borderBottomColor,
    'border-left-color': sourceStyle.borderLeftColor,
    'border-top-style': sourceStyle.borderTopStyle,
    'border-right-style': sourceStyle.borderRightStyle,
    'border-bottom-style': sourceStyle.borderBottomStyle,
    'border-left-style': sourceStyle.borderLeftStyle,
    'border-top-width': sourceStyle.borderTopWidth,
    'border-right-width': sourceStyle.borderRightWidth,
    'border-bottom-width': sourceStyle.borderBottomWidth,
    'border-left-width': sourceStyle.borderLeftWidth
  };
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-font-weight', sourceFontWeight);
  var surfacePalette = {
    'transition': 'none',
    '-webkit-transition': 'none',
    'background': palette.background,
    'background-color': palette['background-color'],
    'background-image': palette['background-image'],
    'color': palette.color,
    '-webkit-text-fill-color': palette['-webkit-text-fill-color'],
    'text-shadow': palette['text-shadow'],
    'border-color': palette['border-color'],
    'border-top-color': palette['border-top-color'],
    'border-right-color': palette['border-right-color'],
    'border-bottom-color': palette['border-bottom-color'],
    'border-left-color': palette['border-left-color']
  };

  // Reset is an anchor while Apply is a button. Keep the filter rail's
  // controls typographically aligned across template-specific font rules.
  var actionResetSelector = [
    '.zsky-filter-actions .zsky-filter-reset',
    '.zsky-search-filter-actions .zsky-filter-reset'
  ].join(',');
  var actionTypography = [
    'font-family', 'font-size', 'font-weight', 'font-style',
    'font-variant', 'line-height', 'letter-spacing', 'text-transform'
  ];

  function syncActionTypography() {
    document.querySelectorAll(actionResetSelector).forEach(function (node) {
      var container = node.parentElement;
      if (!container) return;
      var peer = container.querySelector(
        'button[type="submit"],input[type="submit"],button:not(.zsky-filter-reset),a:not(.zsky-filter-reset)'
      );
      if (!peer || peer === node) return;
      var peerStyle = window.getComputedStyle(peer);
      actionTypography.forEach(function (name) {
        var value = peerStyle.getPropertyValue(name);
        if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== value ||
            node.style.getPropertyPriority(name) !== 'important') {
          node.style.setProperty(name, value, 'important');
        }
      });
    });
  }

  function apply(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 || !node.matches(selector)) return;
    // The full/compact switch is a rounded form with a transparent inner
    // hit target.  Its geometry and surface are owned by theme-mode-switch.js;
    // repainting this button here would turn the inner hit target rectangular
    // again whenever the MutationObserver sees the sync writes.
    if (node.matches('.zsky-theme-mode-switch-hit')) return;
    Object.keys(palette).forEach(function (name) {
      if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== palette[name] ||
          node.style.getPropertyPriority(name) !== 'important') {
        node.style.setProperty(name, palette[name], 'important');
      }
    });
    if (node.getAttribute('data-zsky-language-button-colors') !== '1') {
      node.setAttribute('data-zsky-language-button-colors', '1');
    }
  }

  function applyPalette(node, values, marker) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) return;
    Object.keys(values).forEach(function (name) {
      if (node.style.getPropertyValue(name) !== values[name] ||
          node.style.getPropertyPriority(name) !== 'important') {
        node.style.setProperty(name, values[name], 'important');
      }
    });
    if (node.getAttribute(marker) !== '1') node.setAttribute(marker, '1');
  }

  function applySearchSurface(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 ||
        !node.matches(searchSurfaceSelector)) return;
    applyPalette(node, surfacePalette,
      'data-zsky-language-button-search-surface');
  }

  function applyField(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 || !node.matches(fieldSelector)) return;
    applyPalette(node, node.matches(forumFieldSelector) ? palette : surfacePalette,
      'data-zsky-language-button-control-colors');
  }

  function publishPaletteVariables() {
    var root = document.documentElement.style;
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-background',
      palette.background);
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-background-color',
      palette['background-color']);
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-text-color', palette.color);
    root.setProperty('--zsky-language-button-border-color',
      palette['border-color']);
  }

  function applyTree(root) {
    if (!root || root.nodeType !== 1) return;
    apply(root);
    applySearchSurface(root);
    applyField(root);
    root.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(apply);
    root.querySelectorAll(searchSurfaceSelector).forEach(applySearchSurface);
    root.querySelectorAll(fieldSelector).forEach(applyField);
  }

  publishPaletteVariables();
  document.querySelectorAll(selector).forEach(apply);
  document.querySelectorAll(searchSurfaceSelector).forEach(applySearchSurface);
  document.querySelectorAll(fieldSelector).forEach(applyField);
  syncActionTypography();
  document.documentElement.setAttribute(
    'data-zsky-language-button-colors-ready', '1');

  var pending = [];
  var pendingSet = new Set();
  var flushScheduled = false;

  function queue(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1 || pendingSet.has(node)) return;
    pendingSet.add(node);
    pending.push(node);
    if (flushScheduled) return;
    flushScheduled = true;
    Promise.resolve().then(function () {
      flushScheduled = false;
      var batch = pending.splice(0);
      pendingSet.clear();
      batch.forEach(applyTree);
      syncActionTypography();
    });
  }

  new MutationObserver(function (records) {
    records.forEach(function (record) {
      if (record.type === 'attributes') queue(record.target);
      record.addedNodes.forEach(queue);
    });
  }).observe(document.body, {
    childList: true,
    subtree: true,
    attributes: true,
    attributeFilter: ['style', 'class']
  });
})();

(function () {
  'use strict';

  if (window.__zskyFrontendWidthParityInitialized) return;

  function visible(node) {
    if (!node) return false;
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(node);
    var rect = node.getBoundingClientRect();
    return rect.width > 0 && rect.height > 0 &&
      style.display !== 'none' && style.visibility !== 'hidden';
  }

  function headerRail() {
    var selectors = [
      '.nv2-top-inner',
      'header.wrap.top',
      'header.top.wrap',
      '.zsky-site-header__inner',
      '.zsky-top .inner'
    ];
    var found = null;
    selectors.some(function (selector) {
      found = Array.prototype.slice.call(
        document.querySelectorAll(selector)).find(visible) || null;
      return !!found;
    });
    return found;
  }

  var frame = 0;
  function sync() {
    frame = 0;
    var rail = headerRail();
    if (!rail) return;
    var width = Math.min(
      document.documentElement.clientWidth,
      rail.getBoundingClientRect().width);
    if (width < 240) return;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty(
      '--zsky-public-content-rail', width.toFixed(2) + 'px');
    document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-zsky-width-parity-ready', '1');
  }

  function schedule() {
    if (frame) return;
    frame = window.requestAnimationFrame(sync);
  }

  var initialRail = headerRail();
  if (!initialRail) return;
  window.__zskyFrontendWidthParityInitialized = true;
  // The server inlines this script after the header language controls and
  // before result/detail search markup. Set the final rail in the parser task
  // instead of expanding a 980px fallback in the next animation frame.
  sync();
  window.addEventListener('resize', schedule, {passive:true});
  if (window.ResizeObserver) {
    new ResizeObserver(schedule).observe(initialRail);
  }
})();

(function () {
  "use strict";
  if (window.__zskyThemeModeSwitchInitialized) return;
  window.__zskyThemeModeSwitchInitialized = true;

  var copiedProperties = [
    "background-color", "background-image", "background-position",
    "background-size", "background-repeat", "border-top-color",
    "border-right-color", "border-bottom-color", "border-left-color",
    "border-top-style", "border-right-style", "border-bottom-style",
    "border-left-style", "border-top-width", "border-right-width",
    "border-bottom-width", "border-left-width", "border-radius", "color",
    "box-shadow", "text-shadow"
  ];
  var typographyProperties = [
    "font-family", "font-size", "font-weight", "font-style", "font-variant",
    "font-stretch", "font-feature-settings", "font-kerning",
    "font-optical-sizing", "font-synthesis", "font-variation-settings",
    "line-height", "letter-spacing", "text-transform"
  ];

  function hasGeneratedLabel(style) {
    var content = style && style.getPropertyValue("content");
    return content && content !== "none" && content !== "normal" &&
      content !== '""' && content !== "''";
  }

  function circularControl(rect, style) {
    var radii = [
      style.borderTopLeftRadius, style.borderTopRightRadius,
      style.borderBottomRightRadius, style.borderBottomLeftRadius
    ].map(function (value) { return parseFloat(value || "0") || 0; });
    return rect.height > 0 && Math.abs(rect.width - rect.height) <= 6 &&
      Math.max.apply(Math, radii) >= rect.height * 0.42;
  }

  function labelTextLength(value) {
    // Count only visible letters/numbers.  Legacy language controls often
    // prepend a glyph such as ◎ or use punctuation in a generated label;
    // counting that glyph made two two-character buttons take different
    // geometry even though their displayed labels were the same length.
    return Array.from(String(value || "")
      .replace(/[\s·•…:：/]+/g, "")
      .replace(/[^\p{L}\p{N}\u3400-\u9fff]/gu, "")).length;
  }

  function generatedLabel(style) {
    var content = style && style.getPropertyValue("content");
    if (!hasGeneratedLabel(style) || /^(?:url|attr|counter|counters)\(/i.test(String(content || "").trim())) {
      return "";
    }
    content = String(content || "").trim();
    if ((content[0] === '"' && content[content.length - 1] === '"') ||
        (content[0] === "'" && content[content.length - 1] === "'")) {
      content = content.slice(1, -1);
    }
    return content
      .replace(/\\([0-9a-f]{1,6})\s?/gi, function (_match, hex) {
        var code = parseInt(hex, 16);
        return code ? String.fromCodePoint(code) : "";
      })
      .replace(/\\(.)/g, "$1");
  }

  function visibleTextLength(node) {
    if (!node) return 0;
    var ownLength = labelTextLength(node.innerText || node.textContent || "");
    if (ownLength) return ownLength;
    var before = generatedLabel(window.getComputedStyle(node, "::before"));
    var after = generatedLabel(window.getComputedStyle(node, "::after"));
    var generatedLength = labelTextLength(before + after);
    if (generatedLength) return generatedLength;
    return labelTextLength(node.getAttribute("aria-label") || "");
  }

  function syncOne(form) {
    var strictParity = form.hasAttribute("data-zsky-control-parity");
    var language = form.previousElementSibling;
    while (language && !language.matches("[data-language-modal-open]")) {
      language = language.previousElementSibling;
    }
    if (!language || !language.matches("[data-language-modal-open]")) return;
    var style = window.getComputedStyle(language);
    if (!language.getClientRects().length || style.display === "none") {
      form.style.setProperty("display", "none", "important");
      return;
    }
    var beforeStyle = window.getComputedStyle(language, "::before");
    var afterStyle = window.getComputedStyle(language, "::after");
    var button = form.querySelector(".zsky-theme-mode-switch-hit");
    var labelStyle = style;
    if (hasGeneratedLabel(beforeStyle) &&
        parseFloat(beforeStyle.fontSize || "0") > 0) {
      labelStyle = beforeStyle;
    } else if (hasGeneratedLabel(afterStyle) &&
               parseFloat(afterStyle.fontSize || "0") > 0) {
      labelStyle = afterStyle;
    }
    form.style.setProperty("transition", "none", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("-webkit-transition", "none", "important");
    copiedProperties.forEach(function (property) {
      form.style.setProperty(property, style.getPropertyValue(property), "important");
    });
    var outerTypography = strictParity ? typographyProperties : [
      "font-family", "font-style", "font-weight", "font-size",
      "letter-spacing", "text-transform"
    ];
    outerTypography.forEach(function (property) {
      var value = labelStyle.getPropertyValue(property);
      if (value) form.style.setProperty(property, value, "important");
    });
    form.style.setProperty("padding", "0px", "important");
    var languageRect = language.getBoundingClientRect();
    var height = languageRect.height + "px";
    form.style.setProperty("height", height, "important");
    form.style.setProperty("min-height", height, "important");
    form.style.setProperty("max-height", height, "important");
    var isCircular = circularControl(languageRect, style);
    // Same-length short labels should occupy the same measured button box;
    // longer language labels still leave the mode control content-sized.
    var sameShortLabel = strictParity && button &&
      visibleTextLength(language) > 0 &&
      visibleTextLength(language) === visibleTextLength(button);
    // Normal public mobile pages use an icon-sized 40px language control.
    // Keep the complete/compact peer in that same box; otherwise its label
    // measures a few pixels wider and the header visibly becomes uneven.
    // Forum and member/auth surfaces retain their own independent contracts.
    var inForum = !!form.closest(".forum-shell");
    var inMemberSurface = !!form.closest(".member-wrap,.member-auth-card");
    var protectedFinalTheme = !!(document.body && [
      "theme-zseek", "theme-zseek-green", "theme-zseek-amber",
      "theme-zsky-default", "theme-zsky-muerbt", "theme-zsky-kaixincili",
      "theme-zsky-tbox", "theme-zsky-spider-classic", "theme-zsky-pirate",
      "theme-zsky-nanrencili"
    ].some(function (name) { return document.body.classList.contains(name); }));
    var normalMobile = !inForum && !inMemberSurface && !protectedFinalTheme &&
      window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia("(max-width: 760px)").matches;
    // Non-final templates share the language control's palette, typography,
    // border and height. Outside normal mobile pages, the complete/compact
    // label keeps its own intrinsic width. Matching the peer width there made
    // a two-character mode label as wide as a four-character language label.
    var matchLanguageBox = normalMobile || (!strictParity && isCircular) || sameShortLabel;
    // Non-final templates use an intrinsic mode label.  Do not borrow the
    // language trigger's padding: a late stylesheet/font pass can change
    // that value by 1-2px and make the button visibly jump on first paint.
    // The protected final-ten templates keep their native padding behavior.
    var horizontalPadding =
      (parseFloat(style.paddingLeft || "0") || 0) +
      (parseFloat(style.paddingRight || "0") || 0);
    var modePadding = strictParity
      ? (horizontalPadding > 0
          ? "0 " + style.paddingRight + " 0 " + style.paddingLeft
          : "0 11px")
      : style.padding;
    if (!strictParity && !matchLanguageBox &&
        (parseFloat(style.paddingLeft || "0") +
         parseFloat(style.paddingRight || "0") < 1)) {
      modePadding = "0 11px";
    }
    if (matchLanguageBox) {
      var width = languageRect.width + "px";
      form.style.setProperty("width", width, "important");
      form.style.setProperty("min-width", width, "important");
      form.style.setProperty("max-width", width, "important");
    } else {
      form.style.setProperty("width", "auto", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("min-width", "0px", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("max-width", "100%", "important");
    }
    form.style.setProperty("position", "static", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("display", "inline-flex", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("animation", "none", "important");
    if (language.classList.contains("zseek-mobile-language")) {
      var gap = 6;
      if (!form.dataset.zskyOriginalLanguageRight) {
        form.dataset.zskyOriginalLanguageRight = style.right || "8px";
      }
      var right = parseFloat(form.dataset.zskyOriginalLanguageRight) || 8;
      form.style.setProperty("position", "fixed", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("top", style.top, "important");
      form.style.setProperty("right", right + "px", "important");
      form.style.setProperty("z-index", style.zIndex, "important");
      language.style.setProperty(
        "right", (right + form.getBoundingClientRect().width + gap) + "px", "important"
      );
    }
    if (button) {
      if (strictParity) {
        button.style.setProperty("transition", "none", "important");
        button.style.setProperty("-webkit-transition", "none", "important");
      }
      button.style.setProperty("background", "transparent", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("background-image", "none", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("border", "0", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("border-radius", "inherit", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("box-shadow", "none", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("overflow", "hidden", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("width", matchLanguageBox ? "100%" : "auto", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("min-width", "0", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("height", "100%", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("min-height", "100%", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("padding", matchLanguageBox ? "0px" : modePadding, "important");
      button.style.setProperty("animation", "none", "important");
      button.style.setProperty("color", labelStyle.color, "important");
      button.style.setProperty("-webkit-text-fill-color", labelStyle.color, "important");
      if (strictParity) {
        typographyProperties.forEach(function (property) {
          var value = labelStyle.getPropertyValue(property);
          if (value) button.style.setProperty(property, value, "important");
        });
      } else {
        button.style.setProperty("font-family", labelStyle.fontFamily, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("font-style", labelStyle.fontStyle, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("font-weight", labelStyle.fontWeight, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("font-size", labelStyle.fontSize, "important");
        button.style.setProperty("line-height", "1", "important");
        button.style.setProperty("letter-spacing", labelStyle.letterSpacing, "important");
      }
    }
    form.style.setProperty("transition", "none", "important");
    form.style.setProperty("-webkit-transition", "none", "important");
    form.dataset.zskyThemeReady = "1";
  }

  function syncAll() {
    document.querySelectorAll(
      "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
    ).forEach(syncOne);
  }

  function pairControlsReady() {
    var forms = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(
      "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
    ));
    // A preview intentionally has no persistent complete/compact form.  There
    // is no paired control to wait for in that case, so the language/forum
    // surface must not remain hidden forever.  If the parser later appends a
    // real form, the mutation observer below will run the paired gate again.
    if (!forms.length) {
      return true;
    }
    return forms.every(function (form) {
      if (form.dataset.zskyThemeReady === "1") return true;
      var style = window.getComputedStyle(form);
      return style.display === "none" || !form.getClientRects().length;
    });
  }

  function markPairReady() {
    if (pairControlsReady()) {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute(
        "data-zsky-theme-mode-pair-ready", "1");
    } else {
      document.documentElement.removeAttribute(
        "data-zsky-theme-mode-pair-ready");
    }
  }

  function settlePairs() {
    syncAll();
    markPairReady();
  }

  var layoutObserver = null;
  function observeCollapsibleMenus() {
    if (layoutObserver || !window.MutationObserver) return;
    layoutObserver = new MutationObserver(function () {
      window.requestAnimationFrame(syncAll);
      window.setTimeout(syncAll, 350);
    });
    document.querySelectorAll(
      '#collapse-head,.am-collapse,[aria-controls="collapse-head"]'
    ).forEach(function (node) {
      layoutObserver.observe(node, {
        attributes: true, attributeFilter: ["class", "aria-expanded", "hidden"]
      });
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading" &&
      !document.querySelector(
        "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
      )) {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function () {
      settlePairs();
      observeCollapsibleMenus();
    }, { once: true });
  } else {
    settlePairs();
    observeCollapsibleMenus();
  }
  // The parser-time bootstrap can run immediately after the language source,
  // before the injected mode form is appended. Keep the one-time guard while
  // watching for that later parser insertion so the form does not wait for a
  // deferred duplicate script and flash its native rectangle first.
  var switchObserver = new MutationObserver(function (records) {
    var added = records.some(function (record) {
      return Array.prototype.some.call(record.addedNodes, function (node) {
        return node && node.nodeType === 1 && (
          node.matches(
            "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
          ) || node.querySelector(
            "form.zsky-theme-mode-switch-inline[data-zsky-language-peer]"
          )
        );
      });
    });
    if (added) {
      settlePairs();
    }
  });
  if (document.documentElement) {
    switchObserver.observe(document.documentElement, {
      childList: true, subtree: true
    });
  }
  // Do not resynchronise after the first paint.  Font loading and the window
  // load event used to recalculate the peer width/height and caused the
  // complete/compact button to visibly jump on refresh.  New forms and
  // deliberate viewport/menu changes are still handled above.
  window.addEventListener("resize", syncAll, { passive: true });
  document.addEventListener("click", function (event) {
    if (!event.target.closest(
      '[aria-controls="collapse-head"],[data-am-collapse],.am-topbar-btn,.navbar-toggle,.menu-toggle,.nav-toggle'
    )) return;
    window.setTimeout(syncAll, 0);
    window.setTimeout(syncAll, 350);
  });
}());
